                            "Ja existe um convite pendente " +
                            "para esse jogador."
                        );

                        return;
                    }
                }

                // ------------------------------------------
                // REGISTRAR CONVITE
                // ------------------------------------------

                const invite: Invitation = {

                    id: randomUUID(),

                    partyId: party.id,

                    senderKey: sender.key,

                    targetSteamId,

                    targetInstanceId,

                    expiresAt:
                        Date.now() +
                        OFFLINE_INVITATION_DURATION,

                    delivered: false
                };

                this.invitations.set(
                    invite.id,
                    invite
                );

                // ------------------------------------------
                // TENTAR ENTREGAR AGORA
                // ------------------------------------------

                const delivered =
                    this.deliver(invite);

                // ------------------------------------------
                // RESPONDER AO REMETENTE
                // ------------------------------------------

                client.send(
                    "party_invite_result",
                    {

                        ok: true,

                        delivered,

                        invitationId: invite.id,

                        partyId: party.id
                    }
                );

                console.log(
                    "[PARTY] Convite criado:",
                    invite.id,
                    "| De:",
                    sender.name,
                    "| Para SteamID:",
                    targetSteamId,
                    "| Instancia:",
                    targetInstanceId || "qualquer",
                    "| Entregue:",
                    delivered
                );
            }
        );

        // --------------------------------------------------
        // ACEITAR OU RECUSAR CONVITE
        // --------------------------------------------------

        this.onMessage(
            "party_reply",
            (client, data) => {

                const member =
                    this.memberOf(client);

                if (!member) return;

                const invitationId = String(
                    data?.invitationId ?? ""
                );

                const accept =
                    data?.accept === true;

                const invite =
                    this.invitations.get(
                        invitationId
                    );

                // ------------------------------------------
                // VERIFICAR CONVITE
                // ------------------------------------------

                if (!invite) {

                    this.sendError(
                        client,
                        "Convite expirado ou inexistente."
                    );

                    return;
                }

                // ------------------------------------------
                // VALIDAR DESTINATARIO
                // ------------------------------------------

                if (
                    invite.targetSteamId !==
                    member.steamId
                ) {

                    this.sendError(
                        client,
                        "Este convite nao pertence a voce."
                    );

                    return;
                }

                if (
                    invite.targetInstanceId !== "" &&
                    invite.targetInstanceId !==
                    member.instanceId
                ) {

                    this.sendError(
                        client,
                        "Este convite pertence a outra instancia."
                    );

                    return;
                }

                // ------------------------------------------
                // VALIDAR PRAZO DE 10 SEGUNDOS
                // ------------------------------------------

                if (
                    !invite.delivered ||
                    Date.now() >= invite.expiresAt
                ) {

                    this.invitations.delete(
                        invitationId
                    );

                    this.sendError(
                        client,
                        "O convite expirou."
                    );

                    return;
                }

                // ------------------------------------------
                // RECUSAR
                // ------------------------------------------

                if (!accept) {

                    this.invitations.delete(
                        invitationId
                    );

                    client.send(
                        "party_reply_result",
                        {
                            ok: true,
                            accepted: false,
                            invitationId
                        }
                    );

                    console.log(
                        "[PARTY] Convite recusado:",
                        member.name,
                        "| ID:",
                        invitationId
                    );

                    return;
                }

                // ------------------------------------------
                // ACEITAR
                // ------------------------------------------

                const party =
                    this.parties.get(
                        invite.partyId
                    );

                if (!party) {

                    this.invitations.delete(
                        invitationId
                    );

                    this.sendError(
                        client,
                        "O grupo nao existe mais."
                    );

                    return;
                }

                if (party.launching) {

                    this.sendError(
                        client,
                        "O grupo ja esta entrando numa partida."
                    );

                    return;
                }

                if (
                    party.members.length >=
                    MAX_PARTY_MEMBERS
                ) {

                    this.invitations.delete(
                        invitationId
                    );

                    this.sendError(
                        client,
                        "O grupo esta cheio."
                    );

                    return;
                }

                const sender =
                    this.memberByKey(
                        invite.senderKey
                    );

                if (
                    !sender ||
                    !party.members.includes(
                        sender.key
                    )
                ) {

                    this.invitations.delete(
                        invitationId
                    );

                    this.sendError(
                        client,
                        "Quem enviou o convite " +
                        "nao esta mais no grupo."
                    );

                    return;
                }

                // ------------------------------------------
                // ENTRAR NO GRUPO
                // ------------------------------------------

                this.invitations.delete(
                    invitationId
                );

                // Remover do grupo anterior.
                this.detach(member.key);

                // Registrar no novo grupo.
                if (!party.members.includes(member.key)) {

                    party.members.push(
                        member.key
                    );
                }

                // Atualizar todos os integrantes.
                this.publish(party);

                client.send(
                    "party_reply_result",
                    {
                        ok: true,
                        accepted: true,
                        invitationId,
                        partyId: party.id
                    }
                );

                console.log(
                    "[PARTY] CONVITE ACEITO:",
                    member.name,
                    "| Grupo:",
                    party.id,
                    "| Integrantes:",
                    party.members.length
                );
            }
        );

        // --------------------------------------------------
        // SAIR DO GRUPO
        // --------------------------------------------------

        this.onMessage(
            "party_leave",
            client => {

                const member =
                    this.memberOf(client);

                if (!member) return;

                this.detach(member.key);

                this.createSolo(member);

                console.log(
                    "[PARTY] Jogador saiu do grupo:",
                    member.name
                );
            }
        );

        // --------------------------------------------------
        // ACOMPANHAR O JOGADOR SEM DESFAZER O GRUPO AO SAIR DO LOBBY
        // --------------------------------------------------

        this.onMessage("party_presence", (client, data) => {
            const member = this.memberOf(client);
            if (!member) return;

            const location = String(data?.location ?? "menu");
            const roomId = String(data?.roomId ?? "").trim();

            if (
                location !== "menu" &&
                location !== "lobby" &&
                location !== "playing"
            ) {
                return;
            }

            // O lobby e o grupo sao independentes.
            // Voltar ao menu nao desfaz o grupo.
            member.location = location as Member["location"];
            member.gameRoomId = location === "menu" ? "" : roomId;

            console.log(
                "[PARTY] Presenca:", member.name,
                "| Estado:", location,
                "| Sala:", roomId || "nenhuma"
            );

            // Separar somente o jogador que iniciou a partida
            // sem o restante do grupo, nunca quem ficou no menu.
            const separarSeJogouSozinho = (key: string, expectedRoomId: string): void => {
                const current = this.memberByKey(key);
                if (
                    !current ||
                    current.location !== "playing" ||
                    current.gameRoomId !== expectedRoomId
                ) {
                    return;
                }

                const currentParty = this.partyOf(key);
                if (!currentParty || currentParty.members.length <= 1) return;

                // Integrante que permaneceu no menu nao acompanhou a partida.
                // Integrante em outra sala tambem nao acompanhou.
                const algumFicouFora = currentParty.members.some(otherKey => {
                    if (otherKey === key) return false;
                    const other = this.memberByKey(otherKey);
                    return (
                        other === undefined ||
                        other.location === "menu" ||
                        other.gameRoomId !== expectedRoomId
                    );
                });

                if (!algumFicouFora) return;

                this.detach(key);
                this.createSolo(current);

                console.log(
                    "[PARTY] Integrante separado ao iniciar partida sem o grupo:",
                    current.name,
                    "| Sala:", expectedRoomId
                );
            };

            if (location === "playing" && roomId !== "") {
                const key = member.key;
                const currentParty = this.partyOf(key);
                if (!currentParty || currentParty.members.length <= 1) return;

                const outroJaVoltouAoMenu = currentParty.members.some(otherKey => {
                    if (otherKey === key) return false;
                    const other = this.memberByKey(otherKey);
                    return other !== undefined && other.location === "menu";
                });

                if (outroJaVoltouAoMenu) {
                    // O grupo nao foi junto: separar imediatamente.
                    separarSeJogouSozinho(key, roomId);
                } else {
                    // Quando todos entram juntos pode haver atraso no loading.
                    // Aguardar antes de conferir presencas inconsistentes.
                    this.clock.setTimeout(() => {
                        separarSeJogouSozinho(key, roomId);
                    }, 8000);
                }
            }

            if (location === "menu") {
                // Caso o evento de entrada na partida tenha chegado primeiro,
                // verificar agora quem ja esta jogando sem este integrante.
                const party = this.partyOf(member.key);
                if (!party) return;

                const playingMembers = party.members
                    .map(key => this.memberByKey(key))
                    .filter((other): other is Member =>
                        other !== undefined &&
                        other.key !== member.key &&
                        other.location === "playing" &&
                        other.gameRoomId !== ""
                    );

                for (const playing of playingMembers) {
                    separarSeJogouSozinho(playing.key, playing.gameRoomId);
                }
            }
        });

        // --------------------------------------------------
        // INICIAR PARTIDA
        // --------------------------------------------------
        //
        // Somente o lider pode executar.
        //
        // Este evento e separado de party_invite.
        // --------------------------------------------------

        this.onMessage(
            "party_start",
            (client, data) => {

                const member =
                    this.memberOf(client);

                if (!member) return;

                const party =
                    this.partyOf(member.key);

                if (!party) return;

                if (
                    party.leader !==
                    member.key
                ) {

                    this.sendError(
                        client,
                        "Somente o lider pode iniciar a partida."
                    );

                    return;
                }

                if (party.launching) return;

                const mode: GameMode =
                    data?.mode === "private"
                        ? "private"
                        : "public";

                console.log(
                    "[PARTY] Lider iniciou partida:",
                    member.name,
                    "| Modalidade:",
                    mode,
                    "| Integrantes:",
                    party.members.length
                );

                const requestedRoomId = String(
                    data?.roomId ?? ""
                ).trim();

                if (requestedRoomId !== "" && !/^[A-Za-z0-9]{6}$/.test(requestedRoomId)) {
                    this.sendError(client, "Codigo de sala invalido.");
                    return;
                }

                void this.launch(
                    party,
                    mode,
                    requestedRoomId
                );
            }
        );
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // JOGADOR ENTROU NA PARTYROOM
    // --------------------------------------------------

    onJoin(
        client: Client,
        options: any
    ) {

        const steamId = String(
            options?.steamId ?? ""
        ).trim();

        const instanceId = String(
            options?.instanceId ??
            client.sessionId
        ).trim();

        // ----------------------------------------------
        // VALIDAR IDENTIDADE
        // ----------------------------------------------

        if (
            !/^\d{17}$/.test(steamId)
        ) {

            this.sendError(
                client,
                "SteamID invalido."
            );

            return;
        }

        const name = String(
            options?.playerName ??
            "Jogador"
        ).slice(0, 32);

        const key = this.keyFor(
            steamId,
            instanceId
        );

        // ----------------------------------------------
        // EVITAR DUPLICACAO
        // ----------------------------------------------

        for (
            const existing of
            this.members.values()
        ) {

            if (existing.key === key) {

                this.detach(
                    existing.key
                );

                this.members.delete(
                    existing.sessionId
                );
            }
        }

        // ----------------------------------------------
        // CRIAR REGISTRO
        // ----------------------------------------------

        const member: Member = {

            key,

            steamId,

            instanceId,

            name,

            color: "red",

            character: 0,

            sessionId: client.sessionId,
            location: "menu",
            gameRoomId: ""
        };

        this.members.set(
            client.sessionId,
            member
        );

        // Iniciar com grupo individual.
        this.createSolo(member);

        console.log(
            "[PARTY] Jogador conectado:",
            name,
            "| SteamID:",
            steamId,
            "| Instancia:",
            instanceId
        );

        // ----------------------------------------------
        // ENTREGAR CONVITES OFFLINE
        // ----------------------------------------------
        //
        // Caso um amigo abra o jogo depois
        // de receber uma notificacao Steam,
        // verificar convites ainda pendentes.
        // ----------------------------------------------

        for (
            const invite of
            this.invitations.values()
        ) {

            if (invite.delivered) {
                continue;
            }

            if (
                Date.now() >=
                invite.expiresAt
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            if (
                invite.targetSteamId !==
                steamId
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            if (
                invite.targetInstanceId !== "" &&
                invite.targetInstanceId !==
                instanceId
            ) {
                continue;
            }

            this.deliver(invite);
        }
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // JOGADOR DESCONECTOU
    // --------------------------------------------------

    onLeave(
        client: Client
    ) {

        const member =
            this.memberOf(client);

        if (!member) return;

        this.detach(member.key);

        this.members.delete(
            client.sessionId
        );

        console.log(
            "[PARTY] Jogador desconectado:",
            member.name,
            "| ID:",
            member.key
        );
    }
}
"
friendsroom:"
import { Room, Client } from "colyseus";

import {
    existsSync,
    mkdirSync,
    readFileSync,
    writeFileSync,
    renameSync
} from "node:fs";

import { dirname, resolve } from "node:path";
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";

// --------------------------------------------------
// TIPOS
// --------------------------------------------------

type Account = {
    steamId: string;
    code: string;
    name: string;
    friends: string[];
};

type FriendRequest = {
    id: string;
    from: string;
    to: string;
    createdAt: number;
    // Identifica a instancia do jogo que originou este pedido.
    originInstanceId?: string;
};

type StoredData = {
    accounts: Record<string, Account>;
    requests: FriendRequest[];
};

type JoinOptions = {
    steamId?: string;
    playerName?: string;
    friendCode?: string;
    instanceId?: string;
};

// --------------------------------------------------
// CONFIGURACOES
// --------------------------------------------------

const CODE_PATTERN = /^[0-9A-Za-z]{6}$/;
const STEAM_PATTERN = /^[0-9]{17}$/;

// Usa a mesma conversao Base62 do obj_menu no GameMaker.
// SteamID64 contem o AccountID nos 32 bits menos significativos.
// BigInt impede perda de precisao com SteamIDs de 17 digitos.
const FRIEND_CODE_ALPHABET =
    "0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz";

function friendCodeFromSteamId(steamId: string): string {
    if (!STEAM_PATTERN.test(steamId)) {
        throw new Error("SteamID invalido.");
    }

    let accountId = BigInt(steamId) & 0xFFFFFFFFn;
    let code = "";

    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
        const digit = Number(accountId % 62n);
        code = FRIEND_CODE_ALPHABET[digit] + code;
        accountId /= 62n;
    }

    return code;
}

// Identidades virtuais nao sao contas Steam reais.
// O prefixo nao pode ser digitado como codigo de amigo.
const TEST_ACCOUNT_PREFIX = "dev:";

const DATA_PATH = resolve(
    process.cwd(),
    "data",
    "money_town_friends.json"
);

// Desativar os testes quando o servidor estiver
// configurado para producao.
const DEVELOPMENT_MODE =
    process.env.NODE_ENV !== "production";

// --------------------------------------------------
// BANCO DE DADOS
// --------------------------------------------------

class FriendsDatabase {

    private data: StoredData = {
        accounts: {},
        requests: []
    };

    constructor() {
        this.load();
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // CARREGAR DADOS
    // --------------------------------------------------

    private load() {

        if (!existsSync(DATA_PATH)) {
            return;
        }

        try {

            const raw = readFileSync(
                DATA_PATH,
                "utf8"
            );

            const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);

            this.data = {
                accounts: parsed.accounts ?? {},
                requests: parsed.requests ?? []
            };

            console.log(
                "[FRIENDS] Dados carregados."
            );

        } catch (error) {

            console.error(
                "[FRIENDS] Erro ao carregar dados:",
                error
            );

            throw error;
        }
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // SALVAR DADOS
    // --------------------------------------------------

    save() {

        mkdirSync(
            dirname(DATA_PATH),
            {
                recursive: true
            }
        );

        const temporary =
            DATA_PATH + ".tmp";

        writeFileSync(
            temporary,
            JSON.stringify(
                this.data,
                null,
                2
            ),
            "utf8"
        );

        renameSync(
            temporary,
            DATA_PATH
        );
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // OBTER CONTA
    // --------------------------------------------------

    getAccount(steamId: string) {

        return this.data.accounts[steamId];
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // BUSCAR CONTA PELO CODIGO
    // --------------------------------------------------

    getByCode(code: string) {
        // A conta DEV existe de verdade no banco e e encontrada
        // pelo codigo gerado pela SUA janela, nao pela Steam.
        // Preferir uma conta de desenvolvimento conectada.
        return Object.values(this.data.accounts).find(account =>
            account.code === code &&
            (account.steamId.startsWith(TEST_ACCOUNT_PREFIX) ||
             (STEAM_PATTERN.test(account.steamId) &&
              friendCodeFromSteamId(account.steamId) === code))
        );
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // REGISTRAR CONTA
    // --------------------------------------------------

    register(
        steamId: string,
        name: string,
        code: string
    ): Account {

        const existing =
            this.getAccount(steamId);

        const owner =
            this.getByCode(code);

        if (
            owner &&
            owner.steamId !== steamId
        ) {

            throw new Error(
                "Este codigo ja pertence a outra conta."
            );
        }

        if (existing) {

            existing.name = name;

            // Atualizar registros antigos para o codigo correto.
            existing.code = code;

            this.save();

            return existing;
        }

        const account: Account = {
            steamId,
            code,
            name,
            friends: []
        };

        this.data.accounts[steamId] =
            account;

        this.save();

        return account;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // OBTER AMIGOS
    // --------------------------------------------------

    getFriends(steamId: string): Account[] {

        const account =
            this.getAccount(steamId);

        if (!account) {
            return [];
        }

        return account.friends
            .map(
                id => this.getAccount(id)
            )
            .filter(
                (friend): friend is Account =>
                    friend !== undefined
            );
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // OBTER PEDIDOS PENDENTES
    // --------------------------------------------------

    getPending(steamId: string) {

        return this.data.requests.filter(
            request =>
                request.to === steamId
        );
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // IDENTIDADE VIRTUAL DE DESENVOLVIMENTO
    // --------------------------------------------------

    private getTestAccountId(
        steamId: string,
        code?: string
    ) {
        // Codigo aleatorio cria uma identidade virtual independente.
        // Para o proprio codigo, preserva a identidade de teste antiga.
        return code
            ? `${TEST_ACCOUNT_PREFIX}${steamId}:${code}`
            : `${TEST_ACCOUNT_PREFIX}${steamId}`;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // CRIAR CONTA VIRTUAL DE TESTE
    // --------------------------------------------------

    private ensureTestAccount(
        realAccount: Account,
        code?: string
    ): Account {

        const virtualId = this.getTestAccountId(
            realAccount.steamId,
            code
        );

        const existing = this.getAccount(virtualId);

        if (existing) {
            existing.name = code
                ? `Jogador ${code} (Teste)`
                : `${realAccount.name} (Teste)`;
            return existing;
        }

        const virtualAccount: Account = {
            steamId: virtualId,
            code: code ?? "__DEV__",
            name: code
                ? `Jogador ${code} (Teste)`
                : `${realAccount.name} (Teste)`,
            friends: []
        };

        this.data.accounts[virtualId] = virtualAccount;
        return virtualAccount;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // CRIAR PEDIDO SIMULADO
    // --------------------------------------------------

    private createTestRequest(
        realAccount: Account,
        code?: string
    ) {

        if (!DEVELOPMENT_MODE) {
            throw new Error("Testes desativados no servidor.");
        }

        const virtualAccount = this.ensureTestAccount(
            realAccount,
            code
        );

        if (realAccount.friends.includes(virtualAccount.steamId)) {
            throw new Error("Esse jogador de teste ja e seu amigo.");
        }

        const duplicate = this.data.requests.find(request =>
            request.from === virtualAccount.steamId &&
            request.to === realAccount.steamId
        );

        if (duplicate) {
            throw new Error("Ja existe um pedido de teste pendente.");
        }

        const request: FriendRequest = {
            id: randomUUID(),
            from: virtualAccount.steamId,
            to: realAccount.steamId,
            createdAt: Date.now()
        };

        this.data.requests.push(request);
        this.save();

        console.log(
            "[FRIENDS DEV] Pedido simulado:",
            virtualAccount.steamId,
            "->",
            realAccount.steamId
        );

        return {
            request,
            target: realAccount,
            simulated: true
        };
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // CRIAR PEDIDO DE AMIZADE
    // --------------------------------------------------

    createRequest(
        from: string,
        code: string
    ) {

        const sender =
            this.getAccount(from);

        const target =
            this.getByCode(code);

        if (!sender) {

            throw new Error(
                "Conta nao registrada."
            );
        }

        if (!target) {
            throw new Error(
                "Codigo nao encontrado. A outra instancia deve estar conectada."
            );
        }

        // ==============================================
        // PEDIDO PARA A PROPRIA CONTA
        // ==============================================

        if (target.steamId === from) {
            throw new Error("Voce nao pode adicionar sua propria conta.");
        }

        // ==============================================
        // PEDIDO NORMAL
        // ==============================================

        if (
            sender.friends.includes(
                target.steamId
            )
        ) {

            throw new Error(
                "Esse jogador ja e seu amigo."
            );
        }

        const duplicate =
            this.data.requests.find(
                request =>
                    (
                        request.from === from &&
                        request.to === target.steamId
                    ) ||
                    (
                        request.from === target.steamId &&
                        request.to === from
                    )
            );

        if (duplicate) {

            throw new Error(
                "Ja existe um pedido de amizade pendente."
            );
        }

        const request: FriendRequest = {

            id: randomUUID(),

            from,

            to: target.steamId,

            createdAt: Date.now()
        };

        this.data.requests.push(
            request
        );

        this.save();

        return {
            request,
            target,
            simulated: false
        };
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // RESPONDER PEDIDO
    // --------------------------------------------------

    answerRequest(
        steamId: string,
        requestId: string,
        accept: boolean
    ) {

        const index =
            this.data.requests.findIndex(
                request =>
                    request.id === requestId &&
                    request.to === steamId
            );

        if (index === -1) {

            throw new Error(
                "Pedido nao encontrado."
            );
        }

        const request =
            this.data.requests[index];

        const sender =
            this.getAccount(
                request.from
            );

        const target =
            this.getAccount(
                request.to
            );

        // Validar antes de remover o pedido.
        if (
            accept &&
            (!sender || !target)
        ) {

            throw new Error(
                "Uma das contas nao existe."
            );
        }

        // Remover pedido pendente.
        this.data.requests.splice(
            index,
            1
        );

        // ==============================================
        // ACEITAR PEDIDO
        // ==============================================

        if (
            accept &&
            sender &&
            target
        ) {

            if (
                !sender.friends.includes(
                    target.steamId
                )
            ) {

                sender.friends.push(
                    target.steamId
                );
            }

            if (
                !target.friends.includes(
                    sender.steamId
                )
            ) {

                target.friends.push(
                    sender.steamId
                );
            }
        }

        this.save();

        return request;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // VERIFICAR CONTA DE TESTE
    // --------------------------------------------------

    removeFriend(ownerId: string, targetId: string) {
        const owner = this.getAccount(ownerId);
        const target = this.getAccount(targetId);
        if (!owner || !target || !owner.friends.includes(targetId)) {
            throw new Error("Amizade nao encontrada.");
        }
        const beforeOwner = [...owner.friends];
        const beforeTarget = [...target.friends];
        owner.friends = owner.friends.filter(id => id !== targetId);
        target.friends = target.friends.filter(id => id !== ownerId);
        try { this.save(); } catch (error) {
            owner.friends = beforeOwner;
            target.friends = beforeTarget;
            throw error;
        }
    }

    isTestAccount(steamId: string) {

        return steamId.startsWith(
            TEST_ACCOUNT_PREFIX
        );
    }
}

// --------------------------------------------------
// INSTANCIA COMPARTILHADA DO BANCO
// --------------------------------------------------

const database =
    new FriendsDatabase();

// --------------------------------------------------
// SALA DE AMIZADES
// --------------------------------------------------

export class FriendsRoom extends Room {

    maxClients = 1000;

    autoDispose = false;

    private sessions =
        new Map<string, string>();

    // Uma mesma instancia GameMaker pode abrir varias conexoes FriendsRoom.
    // Todas compartilham o mesmo instanceId/codigo aleatorio de teste.
    private sessionInstances = new Map<string, string>();

    // Identifica qual instancia enviou cada pedido nesta execucao.
    // Necessario nos testes com duas instancias usando a mesma SteamID.
    private requestOriginSessions =
        new Map<string, string>();

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // CRIAR SALA
    // --------------------------------------------------

    onCreate() {

        console.log(
            "[FRIENDS] Sala de amizades iniciada."
        );

        console.log(
            "[FRIENDS] Modo de desenvolvimento:",
            DEVELOPMENT_MODE
        );

        // ==============================================
        // RECEBER PEDIDO DE AMIZADE
        // ==============================================

        this.onMessage(
            "friend_request",
            (client, message) => {

                try {

                    const senderId =
                        this.sessions.get(
                            client.sessionId
                        );

                    if (!senderId) {

                        throw new Error(
                            "Conta nao identificada."
                        );
                    }

                    const code =
                        String(
                            message?.code ?? ""
                        ).trim();

                    if (
                        !CODE_PATTERN.test(
                            code
                        )
                    ) {

                        throw new Error(
                            "O codigo deve ter 6 caracteres."
                        );
                    }

                    const result =
                        database.createRequest(
                            senderId,
                            code
                        );

                    // ----------------------------------
                    // CONFIRMAR ENVIO
                    // ----------------------------------

                    client.send(
                        "friend_request_result",
                        {

                            ok: true,

                            message:
                                result.simulated
                                    ? "Pedido de teste enviado."
                                    : "Pedido de amizade enviado.",

                            requestId:
                                result.request.id
                        }
                    );

                    // ----------------------------------
                    // ENTREGAR APENAS AO DESTINATARIO
                    // ----------------------------------

                    // Nao notificar a instancia que enviou o pedido.
                    // No modo de teste, duas instancias podem usar a
                    // mesma SteamID, mas possuem sessionIds distintos.
                    const originInstanceId =
                        String(message?.instanceId ?? "").trim() ||
                        this.sessionInstances.get(client.sessionId) ||
                        client.sessionId;

                    // Salvar a origem no pedido para o filtro continuar
                    // funcionando mesmo depois de reconectar/reiniciar.
                    result.request.originInstanceId = originInstanceId;
                    database.save();

                    this.requestOriginSessions.set(
                        result.request.id,
                        client.sessionId
                    );

                    this.sendToAccount(
                        result.target.steamId,
                        "friend_request_received",
                        this.serializeRequest(result.request),
                        client.sessionId,
                        originInstanceId
                    );

                    console.log(
                        "[FRIENDS] Origem da solicitacao:",
                        originInstanceId
                    );

                    console.log(
                        "[FRIENDS] Pedido enviado:",
                        result.request.from,
                        "->",
                        result.request.to
                    );

                } catch (error) {

                    client.send(
                        "friend_request_result",
                        {

                            ok: false,

                            message:
                                this.errorText(
                                    error
                                )
                        }
                    );
                }
            }
        );

        // ==============================================
        // ACEITAR OU REJEITAR
        // ==============================================

        this.onMessage(
            "friend_response",
            (client, message) => {

                try {

                    const steamId =
                        this.sessions.get(
                            client.sessionId
                        );

                    if (!steamId) {

                        throw new Error(
                            "Conta nao identificada."
                        );
                    }

                    const requestId =
                        String(
                            message?.requestId ?? ""
                        );

                    const accept =
                        message?.accept === true;

                    const request =
                        database.answerRequest(
                            steamId,
                            requestId,
                            accept
                        );

                    this.requestOriginSessions.delete(request.id);

                    // ----------------------------------
                    // CONFIRMAR RESPOSTA
                    // ----------------------------------

                    client.send(
                        "friend_response_result",
                        {

                            ok: true,

                            requestId,

                            accepted: accept
                        }
                    );

                    // ----------------------------------
                    // NOTIFICAR REMETENTE
                    // ----------------------------------

                    this.sendToAccount(
                        request.from,
                        "friend_request_answered",
                        { requestId, accepted: accept, by: request.to }
                    );

                    // ----------------------------------
                    // ATUALIZAR LISTAS
                    // ----------------------------------

                    this.broadcastFriends(
                        request.from
                    );

                    this.broadcastFriends(
                        request.to
                    );

                    console.log(
                        "[FRIENDS] Pedido",
                        requestId,
                        accept
                            ? "ACEITO"
                            : "RECUSADO"
                    );

                } catch (error) {

                    client.send(
                        "friend_response_result",
                        {

                            ok: false,

                            message:
                                this.errorText(
                                    error
                                )
                        }
                    );
                }
            }
        );

        // ==============================================
        // REMOVER AMIZADE DOS DOIS LADOS
        // ==============================================
        this.onMessage("friend_remove", (client, message) => {
            const ownerId = this.sessions.get(client.sessionId);
            const targetId = String(message?.steamId ?? "").trim();
            try {
                if (!ownerId) throw new Error("Conta nao identificada.");
                database.removeFriend(ownerId, targetId);
                client.send("friend_remove_result", {ok:true, steamId:targetId});
                this.broadcastFriends(ownerId);
                this.broadcastFriends(targetId);
                console.log("[FRIENDS] Amizade removida:",ownerId,targetId);
            } catch(error) {
                client.send("friend_remove_result", {
                    ok:false, steamId:targetId, message:this.errorText(error)
                });
            }
        });

        // ==============================================
        // CONSULTAR LISTA DE AMIGOS
        // ==============================================

        this.onMessage(
            "friend_list",
            client => {

                const steamId =
                    this.sessions.get(
                        client.sessionId
                    );

                if (steamId) {

                    this.sendFriends(
                        client,
                        steamId
                    );
                }
            }
        );
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // JOGADOR ENTROU NA SALA DE AMIZADES
    // --------------------------------------------------

    onJoin(
        client: Client,
        options: JoinOptions
    ) {

        try {

            const steamId =
                String(
                    options?.steamId ?? ""
                ).trim();

            const playerName =
                String(
                    options?.playerName ??
                    "Jogador"
                ).slice(0, 64);

            const suppliedFriendCode =
                String(
                    options?.friendCode ?? ""
                ).trim();

            // ------------------------------------------
            // VALIDAR STEAM ID
            // ------------------------------------------

            if (
                !STEAM_PATTERN.test(
                    steamId
                )
            ) {

                throw new Error(
                    "SteamID invalido."
                );
            }

            // --------------------------------------------------
            // IDENTIDADE DE DESENVOLVIMENTO: CONTA POR JANELA
            // --------------------------------------------------
            const realCode = friendCodeFromSteamId(steamId);
            const explicitInstanceId = String(options?.instanceId ?? "").trim();
            const testMode = DEVELOPMENT_MODE &&
                CODE_PATTERN.test(suppliedFriendCode) &&
                suppliedFriendCode !== realCode;

            // Duas janelas com a mesma SteamID e codigos distintos
            // representam DUAS contas diferentes no banco.
            const accountId = testMode
                ? `dev:${steamId}:${suppliedFriendCode}`
                : steamId;
            const friendCode = testMode ? suppliedFriendCode : realCode;
            const instanceId = testMode
                ? suppliedFriendCode
                : (explicitInstanceId || client.sessionId);

            const account = database.register(
                accountId, playerName, friendCode
            );

            this.sessions.set(client.sessionId, accountId);
            this.sessionInstances.set(client.sessionId, instanceId);

            console.log(
                "[FRIENDS] Identidade:", accountId,
                "| codigo:", friendCode,
                "| dev:", testMode
            );

            console.log(
                "[FRIENDS] Sessao registrada:",
                client.sessionId,
                "| Instancia:", instanceId
            );

            // ------------------------------------------
            // CONFIRMAR CADASTRO
            // ------------------------------------------

            client.send(
                "friend_registered",
                {

                    ok: true,

                    code:
                        account.code,

                    steamId:
                        account.steamId
                }
            );

            // ------------------------------------------
            // ENVIAR LISTA DE AMIGOS
            // ------------------------------------------

            this.sendFriends(
                client,
                accountId
            );

            // ------------------------------------------
            // ENVIAR PEDIDOS PENDENTES
            // ------------------------------------------

            const pending =
                database.getPending(
                    accountId
                );

            for (const request of pending) {

                // Nunca reenviar um pedido de teste legado sem
                // origem rastreavel: isso mostrava o popup ao remetente.

                // Ao reconectar, manter a exclusao da INSTANCIA que
                // enviou, mesmo se ela recebeu novo sessionId.
                if (
                    request.originInstanceId === instanceId ||
                    this.requestOriginSessions.get(request.id) === client.sessionId
                ) {
                    continue;
                }

                client.send(
                    "friend_request_received",
                    this.serializeRequest(
                        request
                    )
                );
            }

            // ------------------------------------------
            // ATUALIZAR STATUS ONLINE
            // ------------------------------------------

            this.broadcastFriends(
                accountId
            );

            console.log(
                "[FRIENDS] Conta conectada:",
                account.name,
                "| Codigo:",
                account.code
            );

        } catch (error) {

            client.send(
                "friend_registered",
                {

                    ok: false,

                    message:
                        this.errorText(
                            error
                        )
                }
            );

            console.error(
                "[FRIENDS] Erro no cadastro:",
                error
            );
        }
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // JOGADOR SAIU
    // --------------------------------------------------

    onLeave(client: Client) {

        const steamId =
            this.sessions.get(
                client.sessionId
            );

        this.sessions.delete(
            client.sessionId
        );
        this.sessionInstances.delete(client.sessionId);

        if (steamId) {

            this.broadcastFriends(
                steamId
            );
        }
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // VERIFICAR SE CONTA ESTA ONLINE
    // --------------------------------------------------

    private isOnline(
        steamId: string
    ) {

        for (
            const id of
            this.sessions.values()
        ) {

            if (id === steamId) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // CONVERTER PEDIDO PARA O GAMEMAKER
    // --------------------------------------------------

    private serializeRequest(
        request: FriendRequest
    ) {

        const sender =
            database.getAccount(
                request.from
            );

        const simulated =
            database.isTestAccount(
                request.from
            );

        return {
            requestId: request.id,
            // Identidade real dentro do Money Town, inclusive em testes.
            steamId: request.from,
            name: sender?.name ?? "Jogador",
            code: sender?.code ?? "------",
            simulated
        };
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // ENVIAR LISTA DE AMIGOS
    // --------------------------------------------------

    private sendFriends(
        client: Client,
        steamId: string
    ) {

        const friends =
            database.getFriends(
                steamId
            ).map(
                account => {

                    const simulated =
                        database.isTestAccount(
                            account.steamId
                        );

                    return {

                        steamId:
                            account.steamId,

                        name:
                            account.name,

                        code:
                            account.code,

                        online: this.isOnline(account.steamId),

                        simulated
                    };
                }
            );

        // Enviar tambem em JSON para clientes GameMaker cujas versoes
        // da extensao Colyseus nao preservam arrays aninhados.
        client.send(
            "friend_list_result",
            {
                friends,
                friendsJson: JSON.stringify(friends),
                count: friends.length
            }
        );

        console.log(
            "[FRIENDS] Lista enviada:",
            steamId,
            "| amigos:",
            friends.length
        );
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // ENVIAR MENSAGEM A UMA CONTA
    // --------------------------------------------------

    private sendToAccount(
        steamId: string,
        type: string,
        data: unknown,
        excludeSessionId?: string,
        excludeInstanceId?: string
    ) {

        for (const client of this.clients) {

            if (
                client.sessionId !== excludeSessionId &&
                (!excludeInstanceId ||
                    this.sessionInstances.get(client.sessionId) !== excludeInstanceId) &&
                this.sessions.get(
                    client.sessionId
                ) === steamId
            ) {

                client.send(
                    type,
                    data
                );
            }
        }
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // ATUALIZAR LISTAS DE AMIZADES
    // --------------------------------------------------

    private broadcastFriends(
        steamId: string
    ) {

        const affected =
            new Set<string>();

        affected.add(
            steamId
        );

        for (
            const account of
            database.getFriends(
                steamId
            )
        ) {

            affected.add(
                account.steamId
            );
        }

        for (const client of this.clients) {

            const connectedId =
                this.sessions.get(
                    client.sessionId
                );

            if (
                connectedId &&
                affected.has(
                    connectedId
                )
            ) {

                this.sendFriends(
                    client,
                    connectedId
                );
            }
        }
    }

    // --------------------------------------------------
    // CONVERTER ERRO EM TEXTO
    // --------------------------------------------------

    private errorText(
        error: unknown
    ) {

        return error instanceof Error
            ? error.message
            : "Erro desconhecido.";
    }
}
"
